customer

agent
The 16-character minimum applies to the new password only. The rules are: - It must have at least 16 characters. - It can't be a common, easy-to-guess or previously compromised password. I didn't find any other composition rules, such as required symbols or numbers. A passphrase of several unrelated words is an easy way to get past 16 characters and is hard to guess. Don't reuse a password from another account. If your person at home can't read you a code, the exception stays active until 11:59 PM tonight. You can also try again tomorrow with your authenticator app. If you run into problems, message us again.

agent
Here are the password rules I can see in our policy for signing in to workstations: - It must be at least 16 characters long. - It can't be a commonly used, expected, or compromised password. Avoid things like "Password2026!", your name, or your company name. A passphrase of several unrelated words works well, for example four or five random words strung together. The policy lists no other composition rules. If the reset page asks for more, follow what it says. I haven't seen anything else, so I don't want to promise it. The exception stays active until 11:59 PM tonight. If someone can reach the phone before your call, it will work. If not, message IT Help again and we'll look at other options. We can't send the codes anywhere other than the HR-record mobile, so that may mean waiting until you have the phone. Good luck with the client call.

Wiki policy doesn't set an order between the police report and telling your manager. It says to tell your manager as soon as possible, so don't wait on the police.
